- **Binding Authority:** [`spec/memory/OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`](OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md)

Mene un tour avec h2a rapide en fable 5.2 + carbon et repose les 6 quesfion a l issi »`
    - `« Tout comme recommandé »`
  - **T-M-C Ownership Rule:** `Template` = `Track` · `Metamodel` = `engram` · `Control` = the `Track` application.
  - **Six Review-Point Decisions:** `1 B` · `2 B` · `3 C` · `4 B` · `5 B` · `6 A`.
  - **Settled Ledger (`D1`–`D34` in [`spec/memory/R15-SEDIMENT.md`](R15-SEDIMENT.md)):** Unchanged; **`D28` is explicitly kept** (`engram`'s live registry stays the canonical reference; `.track/events.jsonl` is **not** an authoritative domain journal written by `Track` control).

| **§4 Point 5 (`:244`)** | **Point 5 — `decided 2026-11-14 (option B)`, `T-M-C`, `D32`** | Stated only that control hooks originate from `engram` and `Track` (`D32`) and run on rules/decision engine (`« api type jev/decison »`), fast LLMs, or control agents. | Marked **`decided 2026-11-14 (option B)`**: control still originates from both sides (`D32`), with each master of its scope—for the `Track` domain, the `Track` application writes and executes its rules (`D1`, template conformance, mandate scope, `D34` replay) and its verdict is authoritative (`engram` records it, never replaces it; without a verdict, a typed write stays `proposed` while raw recording is already in `engram`); `engram` checks metamodel well-formedness and holds the `D7` confidentiality deny-list. |
| **§4 Point 6 (`:245`)** | **Point 6 — `decided 2026-11-14 (option A)`, `T-M-C`, `D24`, `D17`, `D10`** | Described integral local SQLite ↔ remote Postgres transactional sync over `cluster-mesh` (`D24`), `D17=degraded` queueing, and `mcp-platform` routing, without template travel, domain-blind transport, or `Track` conflict rules. | Marked **`decided 2026-11-14 (option A)`**: one bidirectional mechanism enabled workspace by workspace; every typed object cites its template version and digest, and template versions (`Track` and `h2a`) travel with the graph; `cluster-mesh` carries objects without knowing `Track` types; sync never overwrites a `Track`-controlled record, and concurrent template versions published offline are refused on sync so `Track` decides. |

### 6.2 Owner-Decision Conflict & Guardrail Ledger (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`)

1. **`M1` shaped under `T-M-C`, `Point 2B`, `Point 4B`, and `Point 6A` (Zero Closed List of `Track` Types in `engram`):**
   - *Conflict surface:* `rv-spec0-h2a.md` `M1` requested native metamodel support framed around concrete `sentropic.agile-product@1` and `sentropic.programme-wbs@1` vocabularies (`4` ranks, `epicMode: 'build' | 'run'`, `12` stages, `8` statuses, `5x5` risk matrix, `R0–R9` remap). Encoding any closed union of `Track` types, levels, stages, or risk tokens in `engram` would conflict with `T-M-C` and `2B`/`4B`/`6A` (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md:12-13, 22, 24, 26`).
   - *Disposition:* Did **not** place any closed `Track` list in `engram`. Modeled every construct in `DomainPoliciesDecl`, `state_machines`, and `NodeAdmissionPolicyDecl` with open `string`/`number` template parameters; `sentropic.agile-product` and `sentropic.programme-wbs` remain in `Track`'s templates and appear in Spec 0 strictly as non-normative examples.
2. **`B1` and `§7.1 Q1` guarded under `Point 1B` and `D28` kept (`engram` Live Registry Canonical):**
   - *Conflict surface:* Pre-R15 docstrings in `packages/track/src/events/head.ts:18` and `state/snapshot.ts:16` describe `.track/events.jsonl` as "the source of truth", and `rv-spec0-h2a.md` §7.1 Q1 pairs `track.ontology.yaml` with `workspace.ontology-bound` in `.track/events.jsonl`. Treating `.track/events.jsonl` as a primary runtime store or serializer input over `engram` would conflict with **`D28` kept** and **`Point 1B`** (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md:3, 21`).
   - *Disposition:* Applied exact `.track/` wire shapes and `track.ontology.yaml` ↔ `workspace.ontology-bound` replay concordance while explicitly keeping `D28`: `engram`'s live registry is the canonical runtime source feeding `serializeProjectPrSlice`, whereas `head.json` and the snapshot are replay-consistent with `events.jsonl` *within* each materialized per-PR `.track/` slice.
3. **`§7.1 Q5` vs `SPEC_EVOL_TRACK_ONTOLOGY.md:419` guarded under `T-M-C`, `Point 2B`, `Point 6A`, `D28` kept, and `R12`:**
   - *Conflict surface:* While `rv-spec0-h2a.md` §7.1 Q5 removes static `Track*` placeholders from `app-context@2.0.0` via `composed_lineage_pins` + `track-archi2-bridge@1`, `SPEC_EVOL_TRACK_ONTOLOGY.md:419` contained earlier wording specializing `DeliveryArtifact` into eight hardcoded `Track*` classes projected from `track query --format json`. That wording conflicts with `T-M-C`/`2B`/`6A` (no closed `Track` type lists in `engram`) and `D28`/`R12` (`cluster-mesh`-mediated `engram` live registry, not CLI `track query`).
   - *Disposition:* Applied `rv-spec0-h2a.md` §7.1 Q5 (`app-context@1` frozen; `app-context@2.0.0` removes static `Track*` placeholders in favor of `composed_lineage_pins` + `track-archi2-bridge@1`) and did **not** apply the closed 8-class `Track*` list or `track query` CLI projection from `SPEC_EVOL_TRACK_ONTOLOGY.md:419`.
4. **Edit-scope note on `M3` (`writerMandate`):**
   - `spec/memory/contracts/engram-track.md:51` and `spec/memory/contracts/engram-h2a.md:160` still carry `writerMandateId: string` because contract files are outside this pass's edit scope; Spec 0 §1.4, §2.2, §3.2, and §5.1 define the authoritative signed `H2AMandateEnvelope` (`H2AEnvelope<H2AMandate>`) contract matching Contracts M2 (`engram-h2a.md:104-112`, `engram-cluster-mesh.md:50-58`).

### 7.2 Merged Overlap Attribution Ledger (`h2a` vs `sentropic`)

Where both reviews touched the same subsystem, their requirements were merged into a single non-contradictory normative specification with explicit per-clause provenance:

1. **Mandate Wire Envelope, JCS/`Sha256Digest`, Trust Root, and Transport Carriers (§2.3 `:297-395`, §4.2 `:599-600`, §7.1–§7.2):**
   - *From `h2a` (`B1`, `M1`, `OQ-H2A-4`, `OQ-SENT-3` complement):* Real `H2AEnvelope<H2AMandate>` wire shape (`protocol: 'sentropic.h2a'`, `version: '0.1'`, `type: H2AEnvelopeType`, `artifactKind: 'MANDATE'`, `body.kind: 'MANDATE'`) validated by `validateH2AEnvelope` (`:308, :339-374, :388`); single `Sha256Digest` via `computeHash(envelopeSigningView(wireEnvelope))` + lone UTF-16 surrogate rejection in `h2a/src/canonical.ts:11-49` (`:378, :388`); lowercase `'instance' | 'quorum'` from `6A-H1` (`:325, :390, :781-783`); `StpConnectorContext` construction in `sentropic-daemon.ts` / `mcp-central-attachment.ts` (`:302`); local keyring resolution via `local-files/store.ts` (`findInstance`, `listInstanceKeys`) (`:380, :765`).
   - *From `sentropic` (`F-03`, `F-08.3–4`, `OQ-SENT-1`, `OQ-SENT-3`):* NFC + RFC 8785 JCS canonicalization, standard `base64` Ed25519 signature verification over `envelopeSigningView(wireEnvelope)` with `'h2a-mandate-envelope/v1\0'` domain prefix on internal `cluster-mesh` verified-mandate hashes (`:378`); dedicated SPKI PEM `H2AMandateTrustRootPort` (separate from `custody-crypto.ts`) backed by `H2ANhiTrustBundle` locally and 39-auth enrolled bindings on Postgres (`:379-381, :757`); canonical `params._meta['sentropic.h2a.mandate']` + HTTP `X-Sentropic-H2A-Mandate` byte-equality carrier rule (`:382-384, :764`), `BridgeKernel`/`McpWireRunner` `callContext` (`:600`), and out-of-band `StpConnectorContext.h2aMandate` outside closed 14-key A03 `MemoryContextProjectionV1` (`:305, :600`).
2. **Degraded Mode (`D17=degraded`), Session Ingestion, and Neutral Storage/Outbox (§2.1 `:162`, §2.2 `:216, :224`, §2.4 `:399-416`, §2.5 `:417-429`, §7.1–§7.2):**
   - *From `sentropic` (`F-01`, `F-02`, `F-12`, relayed 14/11 `chat-mesh` context):* Prohibition of sidecar `cluster_mesh_memory_outbox` in favor of atomic neutral L3a `scope-outbox-v1` (`memory-projection-outbox`) inside `SqliteKernel.commit(...)` (`:411`); shared SQLite L3a kernel pre-lot `6A-S0b` with `chat-mesh` Lot `B2` and dual-backend `MemoryNeutralHandle` (`:404-405, :730-731`); neutral `session-db` operational turn store cited by `(session_id, entry_id, seq, content_digest)` without duplicating transcripts or altering RC2 `RECEIVER_ROUTE_TABLE` (`:216, :421-423`).
   - *From `h2a` (`M2.2–3`, `OQ-H2A-2`, `OQ-H2A-3`, `OQ-SENT-1` complement):* Local `cluster-mesh` loopback IPC/HTTP daemon target so standalone `registry-bridge` processes never contend on `SqliteEngine`'s `EXCLUSIVE` lock (`:162, :758`); session-front ingestion driven by `runtime/drumbeat/` + `packages/h2a-runtime` alongside `plugin.ts` across all 8 hosts (`:224`); dedicated 8-host synchronous banner helper reading on-demand daemon-cached `/memory/v1/health` with zero polling (`:408, :778-780`); immediate fail-closed refusal on `503 ClusterMeshMemoryDegradedError` with zero secondary JSONL buffering in `~/.h2a/` or `.track/` (`:412, :775-777`).
3. **Legacy Memory Port Retirement (§3.5 `:507-519`, §6.2 `6A-S1d`/`6A-S3` `:734, :736`, §6.3 `6A-H2` `:744`):**
   - *From `sentropic` (`F-02`, `F-05`):* Two-step `sentropic` retirement migrating `api/src/routes/namespaces/memory.ts` and `api/tests/api/cluster-mesh-memory.test.ts` to `compose/memory` first (`6A-S1d`), then deleting `packages/cluster-mesh/src/memory.ts:18-151`, cleaning `src/index.ts:5`, and replacing `graphify-memory-adapter.spec.ts` (`6A-S3`) while preserving `MemoryReplicationPort` (`:1-16`) until Spec 6B (`:509-512`).
   - *From `h2a` (`M3`):* Full `h2a` migration lot (`6A-H2`) deleting the 9 `packages/h2a/src/runtime/memory/*.ts` files, migrating `d11-ceremony.ts:509-517` onto `@sentropic/engram` V2 L0 contracts + `cluster-mesh` MCP, deleting the 9 legacy `test/memory-*.test.js` files, migrating `test/memory-d11-ceremony.test.js`, and updating `focus-kit/test/fixtures/evidence-corpus.v1.json` for `prepack` (`:513-518, :744`).
4. **Seventh Tool (`engram_memory_context`) `Point 2B` Gating & Rollout (§3.2 `:452, :456`, §4.2–§4.3 `:599-641`, §6.2 `6A-S0a`/`6A-S4`, §6.3 `6A-H1`/`6A-H3`):**
   - *From `sentropic` (`F-07`, `F-08.1–2`, `F-11.3`):* Pure L0 `mcp-platform`/`mcp-broker` contract co-freeze (`6A-S0a`) before `6A-E2` (`:147-148, :729`); `createEngramMemoryWireManifest({ contextToolsEnabled })` omitting the 7th tool from `tools/list` when disabled (`:452, :619-620`); `requiredClaims: ['sub', 'tid', 'h2a_context_mandate']` (`:599`); signed L5/P3 `pre-candidate-manifest.json` `.sha256` re-pin + 22 `P09` suites in Gate 2 / `6A-S4` (`:456, :622, :737`).
   - *From `h2a` (`B2`, `M2.1`, `m2`, `OQ-H2A-1`):* Explicit unmerged PR #455 dependency (`fix/registry-bridge-missing-lock` @ `844402eeae1161d74304e634790904785cbb602c`) (`:12, :616, :623`); signed `BridgeLock` / `descriptors.ts` `ContextToolHostSwitchoverPolicy` + `options.memory` preservation in `rebootstrapMissingPluginLock` and `h2a doctor --repair` (`:624-638`); single 7-tool `mcp-central` generation with per-host `shim-bridge.js` / `catalogue.js` masking across all 8 hosts (`:639-640`); 6-tool and 7-tool coverage in `registry-memory*.test.js` (`:743, :745`).

### 7.3 Owner-Decision Conflict & Guardrail Ledger (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`)

1. **`OQ-H2A-1` Guarded Under `Point 2B` (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md:22` — Fixed Switchover End Date Retained):**
   - *Conflict surface:* `Point 2B` mandates that CLI hosts switch one by one *with a fixed end date*, whereas `rv-spec6a-h2a.md` `OQ-H2A-1` warns against hardcoding an arbitrary calendar constant in source code. Making the end date optional or open-ended would conflict with `Point 2B`.
   - *Disposition:* Applied `OQ-H2A-1` by moving `switchoverEndDateUtc` out of source-code constants into the signed `BridgeLock` configuration (`ContextToolHostSwitchoverPolicy` in §4.3 `:626-636`), while enforcing `Point 2B`'s binding rule that a valid non-empty RFC 3339 `switchoverEndDateUtc` remains mandatory whenever `sentropic.engram.memory-wire/v2` is enabled.
2. **`sentropic F-12` (`session-db`) Guarded Under `Point 1B` and `D28` Kept (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md:3, 21`):**
   - *Conflict surface:* The relayed `14/11 20:20` `chat-mesh` rulings assign operational session/stream/blob persistence to `cluster-mesh/session-db`. Treating `session-db` as a replacement for `engram`'s first-rank cognitive session front (`Point 1B`, `D19`) or canonical memory fact registry (`D28` kept) would conflict with `OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`.
   - *Disposition:* Applied neutral `session-db` and L3a `scope-outbox-v1` persistence (*relayed by sentropic, 14/11*) for operational session entries and replication outboxes, while keeping `engram` as first-rank cognitive session-front (`POST /memory/v1/session-front/ingest` recording `engram` anchors citing `(session_id, entry_id, seq, content_digest)` before `Track`) and sole `D28` canonical memory graph authority (§2.2 `:216`, §2.5 `:421-423`).
3. **`h2a M3` (`d11-ceremony.ts`) Guarded Under `R12` (`ARCHITECTURE-RULES.md:5`):**
   - *Conflict surface:* `rv-spec6a-h2a.md` `M3` asks `d11-ceremony.ts:509-517` to replace `./port-v1.js` and `./promote-client.js` with `@sentropic/engram` V2 contracts. Importing any `@sentropic/engram` runtime store or engine subpath in `packages/h2a` would violate `R12`.
   - *Disposition:* Scoped `@sentropic/engram` imports in `d11-ceremony.ts` strictly to pure L0 V2 contract types while routing all `D11` capture/admission execution through `cluster-mesh` MCP (`engram_memory_capture` / `engram_memory_request_admission`), enforced by `6A-H2`'s static `R12` import guard (§3.5 `:516`, §6.3 `:744`).
4. **Edit-Scope Boundary Note (`sentropic F-06.4` & `F-10.2`) and `h2a` PR #455 Path Verification Note (`h2a B2`):**
   - *Contract edit-scope boundary:* `engram-6a-carbon.md` `F-06.4` and `F-10.2` noted residual `providerId: 'engram-memory'` (`spec/memory/contracts/engram-cluster-mesh.md:140`) and 3-member `LineageId` (`engram-cluster-mesh.md:67,84`, `engram-h2a.md:43`). ### 8.2 Merged Overlap Attribution Ledger (`DS` vs `Ontology Registry`)

1. **2D Domain Matrices (`DS Finding 2`, `DS Finding 3`, `DS Finding 5`, `DS-Q2`, `DS-Q4` + `Ontology F5`, `F6.2`, `DOM-Q1`, `DOM-Q2`):**
   - *From Ontology Registry:* `R3` (`856` parts) uses `relation_target` + `registry_order` on both axes with `unassigned_bucket`, `duplicate_occurrence`, and `per_axis_cell`; `R7` uses `node_type` rows (`order: "explicit"`) × `relation_target` `verifies` columns.
   - *From Design System:* Pre-`select` evaluation of `axis.source` against `sourcePin`; canonical `occurrence:cell.<len>...` IDs + cell-disambiguated `accessibleLabels` + entity-scoped peer highlight; omission of `layout` on zero-edge matrices so the DS tabular matrix host renders `R3`/`R7` today while canvas `matrix-grid` remains gated on `ds-layout-hints@2` and refused today.
2. **Hierarchical Folding (`DS Finding 1` + `Ontology F3`, `DOM-Q4`):**
   - *From Ontology Registry:* Per-view scale-appropriate defaults (`collapsed` + `lift_with_summary` on `R1`/`R2`/`R4`, `expanded` on `R5`/`R6`).
   - *From Design System:* Zero pre-projection in `engram`; defaults travel on `viewDocument.groups[].collapsed` alongside retained descendants and `summaryLabels` (`flow-step` exempted), executed by `@sentropic/diagram-core` `projectView` / `summaryCandidates` and interactive `layoutFolded`.
3. **Node Overlays (`DS Finding 4`, `DS Finding 7`, `DS-Q3` + `Ontology F6.1`):**
   - *From Ontology Registry:* Symmetric `OverlayValueSource` `kind: "relation_target"` (`R4` `managed_by` / `operated_by` -> `org_unit`).
   - *From Design System:* Lowered strictly into frozen `460 × 200` `EntityPresentation.text.detail` / `text.footer` (`text_slot`) or DS-token `category_tone` with synthesized `generic@2` legends and mandatory `legend_label` (WCAG 1.4.1), with zero card-anatomy pill/counter additions.

### 8.3 Owner-Decision Conflict & Guardrail Ledger (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`)

1. **`Ontology F1` Closing Remark on Generic Row-Level Hoist vs `T-M-C` (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md:11-15, 21`) & Conductor Ruling:**
   - *Conflict surface:* After proving that stopgap `views:` is per-ontology on `entry.yaml` rather than per-row, `2026-11-14-view-mechanism-s4-review.md` `F1` remarked *"The row-level hoist can remain as a generic tool, but it is not our case."* Retaining an `engram` hoist tool that synthesizes or rewrites `profile.yaml` template declarations conflicts with **`T-M-C`** (`engram` accepts or refuses a template and never writes or synthesizes template content; `F2` likewise assigns `views.<id>` authorship solely to the template author) and with the conductor's explicit fold brief (Item 4: no hoist, `CONFLICTING_FIELD` / `DIVERGENT_NOTES` removed).
   - *Disposition:* Did **not** retain any row-level hoist tool in `engram` (§4.1 `:1089`, §6 Lot V.4 `:1242-1244`); removed the hoist and `CONFLICTING_FIELD` / `DIVERGENT_NOTES` completely in favor of 1:1 template-author declarations.
2. **`Ontology F3` & `F6.2` Guarded Under `§0.1` and `T-M-C` / Points `2B`, `4B`, `6A` (Zero Domain or `Track` Type Lists in `engram`):**
   - *Guardrail surface:* `F3` maps domain catalog kinds (`capability-map`, `roadmap-timeline`, `application-landscape`, `traceability-matrix`) and `F6.2` partitions `R7` rows across 5 verification item node types. Encoding domain viewpoint enums or concrete domain/`Track` node-type lists inside `engram` would conflict with `§0.1` and `OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md:12-13, 22, 24, 26`.
   - *Disposition:* Kept `ViewMechanismKind` strictly structural (`topology | matrix | lane-flow | hierarchy | entity-model`), placed domain viewpoint strings in template-authored `perspective?: string`, and modeled `AxisValueSource` `{ kind: "node_type" }` generically so the ordered type list lives solely inside the declaring template's `explicit_buckets` (`T`), never in `engram` (`M`).
3. **Full Verification Against `D1`–`D34`, `D28` Kept, and `D16` (`archi@1` Unchanged):**
   - Verified zero conflict across all remaining DS and ontology findings: `@sentropic/diagram-core` ownership of `ds-layout-hints@1` (`DS_LAYOUT_VOCABULARY_PIN`) and DS-side folding (`projectView`) reinforce the `§0.1` owner split; `engram`'s commit-pinned live registry remains the canonical reference (`D28` kept); and `archi@1` (`1.0.0`) remains untouched while declarative parity targets `archi@2` (`D16`, Point 4B).

Folded the round-3 Design System re-check (`RECHECK-carbon.md`: `ACCEPT WITH CHANGES`, `Change 1` and `N1`–`N3`) into [`spec/SPEC_ENGRAM_PROFILE_VIEW_MECHANISM.md`](../SPEC_ENGRAM_PROFILE_VIEW_MECHANISM.md) under the binding authority of [`spec/memory/DESIGN-NOTE-VIEW-PROJECTION-BOUNDARY.md`](DESIGN-NOTE-VIEW-PROJECTION-BOUNDARY.md) (view projection boundary between `engram` and the Design System), preserving every owner decision in [`spec/memory/OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`](OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md) (`T-M-C`, Points `1B`–`6A`, `D28` kept) and [`spec/memory/R15-SEDIMENT.md`](R15-SEDIMENT.md) (`D1`–`D34`), as well as every domain ontology registry fold (`F1`–`F6`, `DOM-Q1`–`DOM-Q4`, and the three registry re-check prerequisites in §4.2–§4.3).

### 10.1 Findings & Boundary Rules Applied to `spec/SPEC_ENGRAM_PROFILE_VIEW_MECHANISM.md`

| Source & Item | Section & Post-Edit Lines | Before | After |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`Change 1` + `DESIGN-NOTE-VIEW-PROJECTION-BOUNDARY.md`** *(View Projection Boundary Rule & Removal of Precomputed Fold/Projection State)* | Header (`:11`), §0.1 (`:30, :32`), §1.2.3 (`:309-315, :327-339`), §1.3 (`:572, :574`), §2.1 & `§2.1.1` (`:818, :821, :823-841`), §3.1–§3.3 (`:885, :922, :936-944, :960, :1007-1026, :1057-1071, :1089-1091`), §5 (`:1261`), §6 Lot V.3 (`:1288-1290`) | Emitted static `FoldValidationMask.projectedHiddenRelationOccurrences` on `ProjectedViewEnvelopeV1.foldPolicy` under `folded_edge_policy: "hide"` (which triggers M3 §3.9.1 `relation-target-hidden` errors rather than hiding folded edges, and stales on client toggle), labeled authored `SummaryDefinition` entries as `candidate summaryLabels`, and included fold/occurrence-derived counts (`entityOccurrences`, `foldableGroups`, `defaultCollapsedGroups`, `relationOccurrences`) in `ProjectedViewEnvelopeV1.counts`. | Stated the binding **View Projection Boundary Rule** once in `§2.1.1` (`engram` serves strictly declarations + commit-pinned semantic data; never serves hidden/visible occurrence sets, lifted endpoints, summary candidates, runtime fold states, computed geometry, layout caches, or rendering/fold-derived counts; keeps masks/filters only where they are pure functions of declarations and semantic data) and referenced `§2.1.1` at every affected site. Removed `foldValidationMask` / `projectedHiddenRelationOccurrences` and removed `entityOccurrences`, `foldableGroups`, `defaultCollapsedGroups`, and `relationOccurrences` from `counts`; under `folded_edge_policy: "hide"`, `engram` forwards only `foldPolicy: { interactiveToggle, foldedEdgePolicy: "hide" }` and the DS host dynamically supplies `FoldFilterState.hiddenByFilter` via `hiddenByFold` at the active `collapsed` set. |

| **`F-04` `[BLOCKING]`** *(GitHub Principal Identity & `cluster-mesh/forge` Secret Custody Under `G-NOSECRET`)* | §1.2 (`:134`), §6.1 (`:847-853`), §7.3.2 (`:1521`), §8.2 `1-S0b`/`1-S3a` (`:1559, :1563`), §8.3 `1-H2` (`:1571`), §9 `OQ-S1-5` (`:1588-1589`) | Ingested GitHub comments/issues as `owner.answer` / `owner.request` without verifying Sentropic owner principal binding, and omitted `cluster-mesh/forge` secret custody (`control.github_app_enrollments`) and `G-NOSECRET`. | Specified in §6.1 that: (a) in hosted mode, GitHub ingress resides in `cluster-mesh/forge` + `compose/memory`, PEM & webhook secrets (`current`/`previous`) stay encrypted in `control.github_app_enrollments` under `SECRET_ENCRYPTION_KEY`, webhooks verify `X-Hub-Signature-256` in constant time over raw bytes via `GitWebhookVerificationPort.verify`, and REST backfill uses `GitInstallationLeasePort.acquire`; (b) on local `h2a` workstations, only host `gh` holds credentials outside `engram` and outside Wasm (`G-NOSECRET`); (c) no GitHub event may be classified as `owner.answer` or `owner.request` unless immutable numeric `sender.id` (never mutable `login`) resolves to a verified `(tenantId, workspaceId, githubUserId) -> sentropicOwnerPrincipal` enrollment (`403` or non-owner observation otherwise). |

### 11.2 Owner-Decision Conflict & Guardrail Ledger (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`)

1. **`F-02.1` (`ContentPolicyV1` Transcript Consent) Guarded Under `D24` Integral Workspace Sync & `Point 6A` (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md:26`, `R15-SEDIMENT.md:69`):**
   - *Guardrail surface:* `D24` and `Point 6A` require integral, unmasked same-`(tenantId, workspaceId)` DB-cluster replication once workspace sync is enabled, whereas `sentropic` `F-02.1` notes that local CLI operational transcripts cannot be uploaded remotely without the owner's signed `ContentPolicyV1` (`packages/cluster-mesh/src/sessions/content-policy.ts:89-139`). Treating `ContentPolicyV1` as a blanket mask or stripping `turn_text` on consented synced workspaces would conflict with `D24`, while uploading unconsented raw CLI transcripts would bypass `ContentPolicyV1`.
   - *Disposition:* Reconciled both invariants in §1.3 (`:154`) and §5.2 (`:796`): when a workspace is opted into `D24` remote sync (`Point 6A`) **and** the owner's signed `ContentPolicyV1` authorizes remote session-content capture for that channel, `raw_event` and bounded `turn_text` (`<= 64 KiB`) replicate **integrally and unmasked** (`D24` preserved); when `ContentPolicyV1` withholds remote operational transcript upload, raw `turn_text` bytes stay `syncState: 'local-only'` on the workstation without bypassing `ContentPolicyV1`.
2. **`F-05` & `OQ-S1-5` (`engram-s1-carbon.md` `OQ-S1-3`) Guarded Under `D30` (`R15-SEDIMENT.md:75`, `ARCHITECTURE-RULES.md:7`):**
   - *Guardrail surface:* `D30` establishes external HMI / GitHub / boards as a distinct input layer feeding `engram` first via `cluster-mesh` (`R12`). Dropping non-PR HMI channels permanently would narrow `D30`, whereas keeping `github-issue`, `github-review`, and `project-board` inside Spec 1 `1-S3` is blocked on `sentropic` `origin/main` by `forge` permissions/events, missing `engram-live` contracts, and unverified GitHub↔Sentropic owner identity (`F-04`, `F-05`).
   - *Disposition:* Preserved `D30` three-layer separation in full while scoping Spec 1 Phase 1 (`§1.4`, `§6.1`, `1-S0a`/`1-S0b`, `1-S3a`/`1-S3b`) to the GitHub PR flow (`pr.merged`) + authenticated `session-db` (`owner.answer`, `owner.request`) and explicitly deferring `github-issue`, `github-review`, and `project-board` to a dedicated follow-up `D30` HMI specification.
3. **Edit-Scope Boundary Note (`F-01.2` — `spec/memory/contracts/engram-cluster-mesh.md`):**
   - *Contract edit-scope boundary:* `F-01.2` specifies documenting `POST /memory/v1/session-front/extractions` in Spec 1 `§1.2` and by addendum to `spec/memory/contracts/engram-cluster-mesh.md`. Because this pass authorizes edits strictly to `spec/SPEC_ENGRAM_SESSION_FRONT_AND_EXTRACTION.md` and `spec/memory/Q6-APPLICATION-LOG.md`, `spec/memory/contracts/engram-cluster-mesh.md` was left untouched on disk and the normative addendum is recorded in Spec 1 Header (`:12, :19`) and §1.2 (`:133`).
4. **Full Verification Against `T-M-C`, Points `1B`–`6A`, and `D28` Kept (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md:1-29`):**
   - Verified zero conflict across all 15 findings (`F-01`–`F-15`): `engram` remains first-rank session front (`Point 1B`) and sole `D28` canonical memory authority; `Track` retains template ownership and control authority via `POST /memory/v1/control/verdicts` (`Point 5B`); `h2a` retains role/skill authorship (`Point 4B`); `Point 2B` and `Point 3C` remain untouched; and `R12` + `@sentropic/llm-mesh` mediation (`Point 6A`) is hardened end to end.

---

   - *Finding wording vs real code:* `engram-s2-carbon.md:69,191` suggested replacing `'gate_failed'` with `'GATE_FAILED'` (`errors.ts:53`). Direct read-only inspection of both repositories confirms that `src/mcp/memory/errors.ts:53` is `'REGISTRY_VERSION_UNAVAILABLE'`, that `'GATE_FAILED'` **does not exist anywhere** in either `engram` or `sentropic`, and that the ratified, shipped `PublicMemoryErrorCode` literal in **both** codebases is lowercase **`'gate_failed'`** (`spec/SPEC_MEMORY_MCP_V2.md:516`; `src/mcp/memory/contracts.ts:428`; `src/mcp/memory/errors.ts:66,387-396`; `src/mcp-memory/provider.ts:795`; `packages/mcp-platform/src/memory/contracts.ts:227,237`; `packages/mcp-platform/etc/mcp-platform-memory.api.md:53,650`).

### 15.2 Owner-Decision Conflict & Guardrail Check (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`)

Checked every item (`B1`, `B2`, `M1`, `M2`, `m1`, `m2`, `OQ-S1-1`–`OQ-S1-5`, `N-01`, `N-02`, `N-04`) against [`spec/memory/OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`](OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md) (`:1-29`, `T-M-C`, Points `1B`–`6A`, and `D28` kept): **zero conflicts; every item applied with explicit architectural guardrails:**
1. **`B2` Under `T-M-C`, `Point 1B`, `Point 5B`, and `D28` Kept:** `engram` stays 100% domain-blind (zero hardcoded `Track` `WorkEvent` literals in `engram`), while `engram-live-tracking.ts` and `engram-proposal-evaluator.ts` reside inside `@sentropic/track` (`Lot 1-H4`). `D28` (`engram`'s live registry remains the canonical reference; `.track/events.jsonl` is **not** an authoritative journal written by `Track` control) and `Point 1B` (`engram` produces the per-PR `.track/` projection, `Track` control accepts/replays) are preserved in full, and the single-writer `.track/` rule for work branches is enforced in `h2a` orchestration policy rather than `@sentropic/track` generic skills (`:1037-1043`).
2. **`M1` (`readySlicePayloads`) Under `Point 2B` and `D15=slices`:** Carrying optional `readySlicePayloads` on `RawSessionEventReceipt` serves `D15=slices` non-blocking hook injection during `1-H1` while keeping the 7th MCP tool `engram_memory_context` disabled by default until `engram` + `sentropic` co-signature and `MCP-L5` production qualification (`Point 2B`, `:362-367, :480`).
3. **`OQ-S1-2` (`K_pseudo` Multi-Workstation Sync) Under `Point 6A` / `D24` and `G-NOSECRET`:** Provisioning `K_pseudo` exclusively via `cluster-mesh`'s OS-keychain workspace enrollment key-ring when `D24` workspace sync is enrolled preserves deterministic cross-workstation `D33` pseudonyms without ever storing secrets in `engram` SQLite tables or Git (`:872-877`).

## 16. Spec 3 sentropic review fold

Folded the independent `sentropic` (`@sentropic/cluster-mesh` OWNER, Gemini Carbon) design review (`/home/antoinefa/src/sentropic/.h2a/reviews/engram-s3-carbon.md`, SHA-256 `8ae7dd8d99bab29f0a4d77ba801927cdfed8c52c9f7957c6db6e2cd457d2caf2`, `VERDICT: CHANGES`: `§0` grounding ledger, findings `S3-C-01` through `S3-C-09` with their exact required edits, and `§3` positions on `OQ-S3-1`..`OQ-S3-3`) into **only** [`spec/SPEC_ENGRAM_DORMANT_DREAMING_SYNTHESIS.md`](../SPEC_ENGRAM_DORMANT_DREAMING_SYNTHESIS.md) (`v0.2.0`, `2026-11-15`), governed by [`spec/memory/DESIGN-NOTE-CODE-GROUNDING.md`](DESIGN-NOTE-CODE-GROUNDING.md) (`:1-15`) and [`spec/memory/OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`](OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md) (`:1-29`: `T-M-C`, `Point 3C`, `Point 6A`). Sibling specifications (`Spec 6A`, `Spec 1`, `Spec 2`, `SPEC_MEMORY_FINALIZATION.md`, `plan/08-BRANCH_MEMORY_FINALIZATION.md`, and `spec/memory/contracts/engram-cluster-mesh.md`) were read in full for `S3-C-08` consistency and left untouched on disk in this lot (coordinated route/doc fold tracked in `Lot 3-E5`). `h2a`'s review of Spec 3 has not yet arrived and is not anticipated.

### 17.2 Sentropic-Fold (`41146202`) & Owner-Decision (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`) Conflict / Harmonization Check

Checked every `rv-spec3-h2a.md` finding and open-question answer against the `sentropic` fold (`commit 41146202c1305b258468e37692351f8169c578f3`, `§16` above), [`spec/memory/OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`](OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md) (`:1-29`: `T-M-C`, `Points 1B–6A`, `D28` kept), and [`spec/memory/R15-SEDIMENT.md`](R15-SEDIMENT.md) (`D1`–`D34`). Three overlap points required explicit harmonization so neither `41146202` nor any owner decision was weakened; both positions are logged below:

1. **`B2.4` (`3-S1c`, `memory-dreaming-llm.ts`, & `§7.2` Lot Structure) vs Preserved `sentropic` Fold (`41146202`, `S3-C-01` & `S3-C-07`):**
   - *`h2a` review position (`rv-spec3-h2a.md:38,42`):* Observed an earlier draft discrepancy regarding `3-S1c` and `memory-dreaming-llm.ts` vs `dreaming-shadow-adapter.ts`, proposing either to include `3-S1c` in `§7.2` or to merge `POST /memory/v1/control/proposals` + `/memory/v1/dreaming/*` into `3-S1a`, and to declare published `3-S1a..3-S1c` and `3-S2` prerequisites on `3-H1..3-H3`.
   - *`sentropic` fold position (`commit 41146202`, `S3-C-01` & `S3-C-07`):* Had already resolved that discrepancy by establishing the 6-lot `<= ~150 LOC` single-package `sentropic` split (`3-S1a` manifest/SQLite/local runner, `3-S1b` `memory-dreaming-llm.ts`, `3-S1c` Hono handlers in `compose/memory.ts`, `3-S2a` & `3-S2b` `dreaming-remote.ts` wired onto `WD-2` mesh-native scheduler per `W-D5 B` relayed by `sentropic`, and `3-S3` `api/` `MEMORY_PATHS` with zero cron).
   - *Disposition:* Did **not** collapse `3-S1c` into `3-S1a` (which would break `41146202` and `sentropic`'s `<= ~150 LOC` atomic lot rule `S3-C-07`); kept `41146202`'s 6-lot `§7.2` table (`3-S1a..3-S1c`, `3-S2a..3-S2b`, `3-S3`) intact, added `'dreaming-refusal-extinction'` / `'dreaming.findRefusalExtinction'` to `3-S1a` (`:324, :326, :1049`), and wired `3-H1..3-H4` prerequisites (`:1059, :1065-1068`) directly to `3-S1a..3-S1c`, `3-S3`, and `3-S2a..3-S2b` + `Spec 6B` + `WD-2`.
2. **`M1.3` Live-Store / `.track/` Phrasing vs Binding `D28=engram` Kept, `Point 1B` (`D34=engram`), & `Point 4B` (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md`):**
   - *`h2a` review position (`rv-spec3-h2a.md:56,60`):* Required that dreaming write only memory proposals via `cluster-mesh` and that `packages/track/src/ingest/dreaming-requests.ts` (`3-H2`) remain 100% pure over injected ports with zero `EventStore` / `.track/events.jsonl` access, adding parenthetically that in `h2a` only `h-cond` records in the live store and publishes `.track/` on `main`.
   - *Binding owner-decision position (`OWNER-DECISIONS-2026-11-14.md:17-22,24`, `R15-SEDIMENT.md:73,79`):* `D28` is explicitly kept (`engram`'s live registry remains the canonical runtime reference; `.track/events.jsonl` does **not** become an authoritative runtime store written by `Track` control in place of `engram`), `engram` produces the per-PR `.track/` projection validated/replayed by `Track` control (`Point 1B`, `D34=engram`), and `h-cond` as sole `.track/` publisher on `main` (zero `.track/` writes on work/PR branches) is specifically `h2a`'s repo-local rule carried in `h2a`'s repo overlay (`Point 4B`).
   - *Disposition:* Applied `M1.3`'s exact code boundary in full (zero `EventStore` / `.track/events.jsonl` access in `dreaming-requests.ts`; dreaming writes only memory proposals via `cluster-mesh`; accepted overlays route through `recordHarnessDecisionAndSerializeOverlay` + `overlay-resolver.ts` + reviewed Git PR), while wording `§4.2` (`:756-759`) and `§7.3` (`:1066`) to preserve **both** `D28=engram` + `Point 1B` (`D34=engram`) as the canonical cross-repo architecture **and** `h-cond` as sole `.track/` publisher on `main` inside the `h2a` repository under `Point 4B` (without generalizing `h-cond` as the canonical runtime store outside `h2a`'s repo-local `.track/` governance).
3. **`m2` Opportunistic `openCentralAttachment` / `1-H1` Evaluation vs `sentropic` `OQ-S3-3` (`Zero Extra Daemon in cluster-mesh`):**
   - *`h2a` review position (`rv-spec3-h2a.md:78-83`):* Requested opportunistic `nightly`/`mid-day` evaluation on `mcp-central-attachment.ts` / first `1-H1` ingest for standalone `agy` and `muse` CLI hosts (`push: false` in `plugin.ts:108,129`).
   - *`sentropic` fold position (`commit 41146202`, `OQ-S3-3` `Spec 3 :125, :267, :1086`):* Required zero extra background daemon or polling loop inside `@sentropic/cluster-mesh` for `mid-day`.
   - *Disposition:* Fully compatible and folded together (`§1.1 :124-125`, `§1.2 :258-260, :267`, `§7.3 :1065`): evaluating `nightly`/`mid-day` opportunistically on host-side `openCentralAttachment` / first `1-H1` ingress requires zero daemon or timer loop inside `cluster-mesh`.

### 18.4 Pending owner dossier

Checked `Spec 4` (`v0.2.0`) for consistency against the owner direction of `2026-11-15` (quoted below for consistency checking only; `Spec 4` was **not** redesigned around it and none of the points below are resolved here):

> « Deja engram doit devenir un loguer de session en // des outils. Dans notre outil cli a nous ce deviendra le loggeur principal. La session est loguée en graph. Et donc seul le diff est transmis a chaque fois. »

1. **Full Contextual Slice Snapshot per Turn vs Session-as-Graph & Diff-Only Transmission (`« La session est loguée en graph. Et donc seul le diff est transmis a chaque fois. »`):**
   - `Spec 4` (`Dependencies :17`, `§1.2.1 :122`, `§2.1 :297-298`, `§2.3 :389`, `§3.3 :555`, `§5.1 4-E4 :839`) specifies `engram_memory_context` (`Spec 2`) returning full snapshot payloads for `roleConsciousnessSlice` (`activeRoleId`, `allowedActivities`, `multiActorRelations`, `roleTemplateEnvelope`) and `harnessEffectiveRules` (`MasterHarness ⊓ repo ⊓ workspace ⊓ worktree`) on each turn, rather than modeling the active session as a live graph and transmitting **only the graph diff** since the prior turn/cursor (where unchanged role, mandate, and overlay bindings between consecutive turns would transmit zero redundant nodes/edges).
2. **Uniform External-Host Hook Window Ingestion vs Parallel Session Graph Logger Alongside Third-Party Tools + Primary Logger in Our Own CLI (`« Deja engram doit devenir un loguer de session en // des outils. Dans notre outil cli a nous ce deviendra le loggeur principal. »`):**
   - `Spec 4` (`§1.3 :166`, `§2.1 :271-274, :286`, `§2.2 :309-313`) models unassigned-role detection and `D16` activity scoring uniformly over `h2a` host-hook per-event pushes (`POST /memory/v1/session-front/ingest` scanning a flat `<= 32` `raw_event` journal window across the 8 external CLI hosts), without distinguishing (a) `engram` operating as a parallel session graph logger alongside third-party CLI tools from (b) `engram` serving directly as the **primary** session graph logger inside our own CLI, where role/scope analysis would consume incremental session-graph diffs natively rather than reconstructing activity windows from hook events.
3. **Full-Envelope `mandate_binding` & Full-Snapshot Overlay Transport Across Session Boundaries vs Graph-Diff Propagation:**
   - `Spec 4` (`§2.1 :301`, `§2.3 :394`, `§3.4 :640`) transmits full `mandate_binding` envelopes on session re-binding and full `SerializedH2aHarnessOverlayV1` snapshots across session boundaries, whereas under a graph-logged session with diff-only wire transmission, session-to-`engram` and `engram`-to-session transitions (mandate attachment/revocation, `D16` advisory proposal emission, and `harness_decision` additions) would be exchanged strictly as incremental graph diffs against the prior session epoch, reserving full deterministic JCS file projection solely for Git `.h2a/overlays/**` commits (`D31`, `D34`).

---

### 19.3 Code-grounding rules (`Rules 1–5`), owner working rules (`2026-11-15`), and round-1 grounding ledger

#### Code-grounding verification rules (`Rules 1–5` — verbatim from `97504c0d:spec/memory/DESIGN-NOTE-CODE-GROUNDING.md:5-15`)

Every statement about existing code in a spec is EVIDENCE, or it is marked as new.
1. **An anchor** (`path:line`) is written only after reading that exact line in the target repository, at a named commit (`origin/main` of that repo, by SHA). Line ranges are re-read after each edit of the spec.
2. **A cited script, Make target, constant, type, function or error code** must exist. Prove it with a command run during the fold: `git grep`, `rg` or `ls`, recording what the command is and its hit. If it does not exist, the spec says « new », names the lot that adds it, and never cites it as existing.
3. **« Wired » or « called » claims** need the call site on the real (non-test) path, as an anchor. A function only called from tests is « not wired ».
4. **Schema claims** (JSON Schema dialects, bounded parameter grammars) are checked against the validator that enforces them: the grammar's own rules (for example `$ref` and open objects being forbidden) bind the spec.
5. **Identity of signers, keys and gates** is taken from the code or registry that defines them, never from a summary.

Process:
- The fold runs the checks of rules 1–5 and appends a « Grounding ledger » to the fold log. Each row records: claim → command → result → anchor or « new ».
- Any claim that cannot be grounded is removed or rewritten as « new ».

     1. **`F4` `choice` Option Shape & Verdict Accessor (`engram-s4-carbon.md:107,161` vs `sentropic@913e3bcbc9de`):** The review sketch wrote `options: candidates.map(({ roleId, label }) => ({ id: roleId, label }))` (`:161`) and `optionId: string` (`:107`). On `sentropic` `origin/main`, `packages/llm-mesh/src/decision/request.ts:31-32` and `criterion.ts:28-30` enforce `hasOnlyKeys(entry, ['id', 'description']) && isText(entry.description)` (so `{ id, label }` fails `parseDecisionRequest` with `'REQUEST_INVALID'`), `request-policy.ts:12-17` requires `raw?: RawPolicy` (`{ mode: 'none' }`), and `verdict.ts:17-28` exposes the selected option ID on `verdict.label?.value` (`{ value: string; origin: 'provider' } | null`). `Spec 4` (`:365, :375, :379, :938, :964`) specifies `{ id: roleId, description }`, `raw: { mode: 'none' }`, and `verdict.label?.value`.
     2. **`F5.3` / `OQ-S4-2` Descriptor File & Qualification Error Code (`engram-s4-carbon.md:113,176` vs `sentropic@913e3bcbc9de`):** The review cited `openAiDecisionsDescriptor` at `descriptor.ts:85-89` and `code: 'CANDIDATE_UNQUALIFIED'`. On `sentropic` `origin/main`, `descriptor.ts` (`89` lines) defines `jevDescriptor` (`servedIdentity: 'unqualified'` at `:76`), `openAiDecisionsDescriptor` is defined in `packages/llm-mesh/src/decision/openai-descriptor.ts:8-28` (`servedIdentity: 'unqualified'` at `:11`, re-exported by `decision/host.ts:13`), and `validateDecisionPolicy` (`policy.ts:11-17`, calling `canExecuteDecisionCandidate` at `qualification.ts:86-88`) returns `'PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE'` (`errors.ts:8`). `Spec 4` (`:351, :938, :964, :992`) anchors both descriptors and `'PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE'` to those exact files and lines.
     3. **`Spec 6A` Lot Owning `packages/cluster-mesh/src/compose/memory.ts` (`engram-s4-carbon.md:40,90,231` vs `Spec 6A:747-750`):** The review attributed `src/compose/memory.ts` to `6A-S1a`, whereas `Spec 6A §6.2` (`:747-750`) places `memory-binding-v2` / `MemoryNeutralHandle` in `6A-S1a` (`:747`), `h2a-mandate-verifier.ts` in `6A-S1b` (`:748`), `src/compose/memory.ts` in `6A-S1c` (`:749`), and `api/src/routes/namespaces/memory.ts` in `6A-S1d` (`:750`). `Lot 4-S1a` (`:936`) therefore declares `6A-S0b, 6A-S1a, 6A-S1c`, and `Lot 4-S1c` (`:937`) declares `4-S1a, 6A-S1b`.
     4. **`F9` `Lot 4-S2` Test Path (`97504c0d` `Spec 4:851` vs `engram-s4-carbon.md:214`):** At `97504c0d`, `Spec 4:851` already used `api/tests/api/memory-h2a-lineage-routes.test.ts` (whereas `engram-s4-carbon.md:214` read it as `api/tests/routes/memory-h2a-lineage.test.ts`); `Spec 4` (`:49, :939, :993`) adopts the exact `F9` filename `new api/tests/api/memory-h2a-lineage.test.ts` inside `api/tests/api/` plus the exact `F9` free-port and `; make down ENV=test-api-s4` verification gate.

## 24. View grammar freeze fixes

Executed on `2026-11-15` (`Stage: DESIGN`, `Gemini Carbon`) on branch `docs/memory-r15-sediment` (`HEAD = 583947e7904512f32372ae404f40f3047ce0ff1b`), resolving the Design System freeze review (`scratchpad/engram-view/FREEZE-carbon.md`, `VERDICT: FREEZE-BLOCKED`, `sha256:179d065668c6c3473cfa8bb14edcbb62562381312e48caefc80ebdd7783155ab`: blockers `F1`–`F3` and non-blocking `F4`) against published `@sentropic/diagram-core` `0.3.0` and `@sentropic/diagram-canvas` `0.2.4` (`sent-tech-design-system` tags `diagram-core-v0.3.0` and `diagram-canvas-v0.2.4` at `234e090bebe6e64109aa79bade9e54901936b1b7`, byte-identical to `origin/main` `b7dc7d1a6e8f862c8d0dc0a4c2d7519be40fd67b` across `packages/diagram-core`, `packages/diagram-canvas`, and `packages/tokens`) and the Design System `hints@2` plan (`ds-r7kit.md` `sha256:940da9f3c74549ee56f3c7cada94cb60d8b998c5587fb6280c2efc1ff4e5728a`, `ds-position-engine.md` `sha256:96d8eac0e2ed4a6d09094f4de1088ab4d8dfbc288832386f910bb524b0eb0892`). Edited **only** [`spec/SPEC_ENGRAM_PROFILE_VIEW_MECHANISM.md`](../SPEC_ENGRAM_PROFILE_VIEW_MECHANISM.md) and [`spec/memory/Q6-APPLICATION-LOG.md`](Q6-APPLICATION-LOG.md) (zero commits, zero code edits, zero `1.x` DS revision requested).

## 25. View grammar freeze fixes, round 2

Executed on `2026-11-16` (`Stage: DESIGN`, `Gemini Carbon`) on branch `docs/memory-r15-sediment` (`HEAD = 0e734e09`), resolving the Design System round-2 freeze review (`/home/antoinefa/src/sent-tech-design-system/scratchpad/engram-view/FREEZE-opus-r2.md`, `VERDICT: FREEZE-BLOCKED`, `sha256:b244eed327d7405c662a13d42fbfb54bc6d98ddfae241406240df7dafe718ad5`: blocker `B1`, `M1`–`M4`, `I1`–`I2`) against published `@sentropic/diagram-core` `0.3.0` (`diagram-core-v0.3.0` = `234e090bebe6e64109aa79bade9e54901936b1b7`, `0` diff bytes to `origin/main` `27a8297441a2fd2e5aa97977dedff4723cdc4c02`) and `@sentropic/diagram-canvas` `0.2.4` / `0.2.5` (`diagram-canvas-v0.2.4` = `234e090bebe6e64109aa79bade9e54901936b1b7`, `diagram-canvas-v0.2.5` = `27a8297441a2fd2e5aa97977dedff4723cdc4c02`). Edited **only** [`spec/SPEC_ENGRAM_PROFILE_VIEW_MECHANISM.md`](../SPEC_ENGRAM_PROFILE_VIEW_MECHANISM.md) and [`spec/memory/Q6-APPLICATION-LOG.md`](Q6-APPLICATION-LOG.md) (zero commits, zero code edits, zero Python).

### 25.0 Design Note — V1 Consumer-Existence Rule Across the Whole Grammar (Written Before Editing)

Round-1 finding `F1` returned `PARTIAL` in round 2 (`B1`) because round-1 fix step 4 was omitted: vocabulary strings (`lane-stack`, `direction`, `rotate_labels`) were removed from V1 prose, but grammar values whose only rendering path depended on them (`kind: "lane-flow"`, `axes.mode: "bands_1d"`) stayed admitted in V1, silently degrading to packed cards under `mode: "auto-pack"`.

**Binding V1 Consumer-Existence Rule (closes this defect class permanently):**
- **Every value V1 (`ds-layout-hints@1`) admits MUST have an active consumer in the published `@sentropic/diagram-core@0.3.0` (`ds-layout-hints@1`) / `@sentropic/diagram-canvas` `0.2.4+` (`0.2.4` / `0.2.5`) chain** (or the DS tabular matrix host for zero-edge tabular `matrix` views, `DS-Q2`);
- **Otherwise V1 MUST refuse it with an existing `MM_VIEW_*` diagnostic (keeping the diagnostic catalog fixed at `23` codes) and reserve the value in the TypeScript schema for `ds-layout-hints@2` (`hints@2` / DC-2d)** so enabling it later remains an additive `MINOR` change.

Auditing **every closed union, sub-structure, and value across the whole V1 grammar** against `sent-tech-design-system` `origin/main` (`27a829744`) surfaces the two values named in `FREEZE-opus-r2.md` (`B1`) **plus five additional unconsumed V1 values/combinations**:

1. **Named in `FREEZE-opus-r2.md` (`B1`):**
   - `ViewMechanismKind`: `kind: "lane-flow"` — requires `lane-stack` (`DS_LAYOUT_FUTURE_MODES` in `packages/diagram-core/src/layout-hints.ts:39, 453-456`) or DC-2d `resolveNotation` (`packages/diagram-core/src/notation.ts:19-22`, absent in `0.3.0`); `@sentropic/diagram-canvas` `0.2.4` / `0.2.5` has zero `"lane"`, `packLaneStack`, or `bands_1d` consumer. **Reserved for `ds-layout-hints@2`; refused in `@1` with `MM_VIEW_AXES_KIND_MISMATCH`.**
   - `ViewAxesDecl`: `axes.mode: "bands_1d"` (together with `axesProjection.mode: "bands_1d"` and `occurrence:band.*` IDs) — `lowerLayoutHints` lowers `grouping_axis` to a single root group (`packages/diagram-core/src/layout-hints.ts:544, 568-573`), never per-band groups, and `layoutHint` is forwarded untouched. **Reserved for `ds-layout-hints@2`; refused in `@1` on every view kind with `MM_VIEW_AXES_KIND_MISMATCH`.**
2. **Additional V1 values found by whole-grammar audit and closed under the same rule:**
   - **(a) `ViewMechanismKind`: `kind: "entity-model"`** — `@sentropic/diagram-canvas` `0.2.4` / `0.2.5` dispatches `layoutErd` (`packages/diagram-canvas/src/layout/measured.ts:264`, `src/layout/erd.ts:8`) and `measureErdCard` / `renderErdCardSvg` (`packages/diagram-canvas/src/presentation/diagramSceneModel.ts:112, 365, 479, 506`, `src/presentation/erd.ts:79, 99`) **only** when `document.profileRefs` is `["erd@1"]` (`packages/diagram-core/src/profiles/erd.ts:2, 5-30`), whereas `engram` Stage 6 compiles template-owned `ProfileId`s (`<templateId>@<major>`) and `resolveNotation` for `diagram-notation@1` `role: "entity-table"` is absent in `0.3.0` (`packages/diagram-core/src/notation.ts:19-22`). Under `@1` `auto-pack`, `entity-model` silently degrades to packed `460 × 200` `DiagramCard`s. **Reserved for `ds-layout-hints@2` (with DC-2d `resolveNotation`); refused in `@1` with `MM_VIEW_KIND_INVALID`.**
   - **(b) `OverlayChannel`: `channel: "provisional_marker"` (and `ViewOverlayDecl.equals`)** — Stage 5 lowers `provisional_marker` solely into `ProjectedViewEnvelopeV1.notationBlocks[profileId].relationTypes[relTypeId].provisional` (`packages/diagram-core/src/notation.ts:64-72, 141, 242`). Neither `@sentropic/diagram-core@0.3.0` (`notation.ts:19-22`) nor `@sentropic/diagram-canvas` `0.2.4` / `0.2.5` (`SceneLayoutInput` in `packages/diagram-canvas/src/presentation/diagramSceneModel.ts:126-146`, `renderSceneSvg.ts:9-22`; `0` matches for `provisional`, `data-provisional`, or `NotationBlock` in `packages/diagram-canvas/src`) reads `notationBlocks`, so the overlay is silently dropped at render time (including on Specimen C). **Reserved for `ds-layout-hints@2` (with DC-2d `resolveNotation`); refused in `@1` with `MM_VIEW_OVERLAY_RAW_STYLE_FORBIDDEN` (alongside non-V1 channels `"badge"`, `"detail_pill"`, `"metric_counter"`), and removed from V1 Specimen C.** *(By contrast, `channel: "stroke_style"` has active `@1` consumers in `validateDiagramPresentation` at `presentation-validate.ts:244-247`, `projectView` / `summaryCandidates` / `analyzeSummaries` at `fold.ts:247, 280, 574, 667, 782, 799`, and `diagram-canvas` `planFlows` at `layout/flows.ts:62` + `measured.ts:2143`, and is retained in V1.)*
   - **(c) `diagram-notation@1` roles `"lane"`, `"entity-table"`, `"flow-node"` and `NotationRelation` `encoding: "detail" | "footer"`, `provisional`, `lift`, `fallback` on selected view types/relations** — Under `presentation_vocabulary: "diagram-notation@1"`, `EntityPresentation.form` (`packages/diagram-core/src/presentation.ts:25`) and `@sentropic/diagram-canvas` `0.2.4` / `0.2.5` (`diagramSceneModel.ts:366-376`, `renderSceneSvg.ts:23-32`) execute **only** node roles `"card"` and `"container"` and relation encodings `"arrow"` and `"containment"`; `"lane"`, `"entity-table"`, `"flow-node"` (compact face in DS Lot L1, `ds-r7kit.md:51, 78-84`), and notation-level `detail` / `footer` / `provisional` / `lift` / `fallback` (`packages/diagram-core/src/notation.ts:31, 34, 64-72`) have no consumer without `resolveNotation` (`notation.ts:19-22`). **Reserved on selected view types/relations for `ds-layout-hints@2` / DC-2d `resolveNotation`; refused in `@1` with `MM_VIEW_NOTATION_UNCOVERED`.**
   - **(d) Foreign `<importAlias>` entries in `select.lineages` and qualified `<importAlias>:<id>` refs** — `@sentropic/diagram-core@0.3.0` `validateDocument` (`packages/diagram-core/src/validate.ts:573-597`) rejects every cross-profile relation endpoint under `scoped-constraints@1` with `endpoint-without-join` (`Profile.requires` and `RelationRoleDeclaration.entityTypeRefs` are absent in `packages/diagram-core/src/profile.ts:59-66, 227-240`), causing `@sentropic/diagram-canvas` `0.2.4` / `0.2.5` `layoutMeasured` (`packages/diagram-canvas/src/layout/measured.ts:331-337`) to refuse the document (`document-invalid`). **Reserved for `ds-layout-hints@2` / DC-2d L2 (`Profile.requires` + `entityTypeRefs`); `@1` restricts `select.lineages` to `["self"]` and unqualified local refs, refusing foreign `<importAlias>` refs with `MM_VIEW_LINEAGE_UNPINNED`.**
   - **(e) `folds` (`ViewFoldDecl`) on `kind === "matrix"` in `@1`** — `@1` `matrix` views are strictly zero-edge tabular (`select.relations.include_types: []`, `layout === undefined`), rendered by the DS tabular matrix host (`DS-Q2`), which has no container-fold consumer (`packMatrixGrid` + folded cross-cell matrices belong to `ds-layout-hints@2`). **Reserved on `matrix` for `ds-layout-hints@2`; refused in `@1` (`folds !== undefined` when `kind === "matrix"`) with `MM_VIEW_AXES_KIND_MISMATCH`.**

### 25.2 Grounding Ledger (`Rules 1–5` of `§19.3 :684-697`)
